# Bekko Embedding v1 Unsupervised Training Data

This is the unsupervised training dataset used for the `bekko-embedding-v1` embedding model family. It contains pair and triplet examples for embedding pretraining, published as Hugging Face dataset subsets so that each source/subset/task combination can be loaded independently.

For the full training recipe and technical details, see [Bekko Embedding: Parameter-Efficient Multilingual Retrieval with Ultra-Compact Encoders](https://huggingface.co/papers/2607.25180).

The dataset contains 2,452 subsets and 1.15B rows in total. The rows are pair or triplet examples for embedding pretraining, not human relevance annotations. The source text comes from the upstream datasets listed below and is normalized into `query`, `pos`, and sometimes `neg` fields for embedding training.

## Structure

Each subset uses one of two schemas:

| Schema | Columns | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| Pair | `shared_id`, `query`, `pos` | A positive pair. `query` may be an actual search query, a title, an anchor sentence, a prompt, or the first document depending on the source. `pos` is the paired positive document/text. |
| Triplet | `shared_id`, `query`, `pos`, `neg` | A query/anchor, a positive text, and one hard or contrastive negative text. |

`shared_id` is a deterministic identifier included for row grouping and reproducibility within this release. It is not an upstream document id and should not be treated as a stable id outside this released snapshot.

Most subsets have only a `train` split. Some upstream sources expose additional splits, for example the FineWeb2-IR triplet subsets include `train`, `validation`, and `test` data files. Empty upstream splits are preserved when present.

## Subset naming

Subset names follow:

```text
{source_slug}__{subset_slug}__{dd or qd}{__triplet}
```

- `source_slug` is the source dataset name with `/` converted to `-`, lowercased and sanitized. For example `hotchpotch/FineWeb2-IR` becomes `hotchpotch-fineweb2-ir`.
- `subset_slug` is the upstream subset name after sanitization. For language-pair bitext this is often a language-pair id such as `ace_Latn-ban_Latn`; for Wikipedia it can be a dump id such as `enwiki-20251202-v1.1.0`.
- `qd` means query-document. The first text is intended to behave like a query, title, prompt, or search intent.
- `dd` means document-document. Both sides are document/sentence-like texts such as translations, related paragraphs, or NLI sentence pairs.
- `__triplet` is appended when the subset has a `neg` column.

Examples:

- `hotchpotch-fineweb2-ir__eng_Latn__qd__triplet`
- `hotchpotch-nllb-sampled-500k-without-hq__ace_Latn-ban_Latn__dd`
- `huggingfacefw-finewiki__ja__qd`
- `sentence-transformers-all-nli__triplet__dd__triplet`

## Token length processing

This release contains the processed text used for training. Most large text sources were truncated during preprocessing with the mmBERT tokenizer (`hotchpotch/mmBERT-L13H384-pruned`) to a maximum of 512 tokens for the active text fields (`query`, `pos`, and `neg` where applicable). Some upstream datasets are already short or use source-specific preprocessing, so users should treat 512 tokens as the dominant processing rule rather than a universal guarantee for every single row.

## Source labels and upstream datasets

The config name starts with a source label. In most cases this is the same as the Hugging Face dataset repo. In a few cases the label is a more specific release label used in this dataset card, while the loaded upstream dataset is shown separately below. `Rows` counts examples in this release.

| Source label in config names | Upstream dataset loaded | Configs | Rows |
|---|---|---:|---:|
| `HuggingFaceFW/finewiki` | [HuggingFaceFW/finewiki](https://huggingface.co/datasets/HuggingFaceFW/finewiki) | 314 | 37.4M |
| `hotchpotch/FineWeb2-IR` | [hotchpotch/FineWeb2-IR](https://huggingface.co/datasets/hotchpotch/FineWeb2-IR) | 21 | 55.4M |
| `hotchpotch/arxiv-ir-simulated-search-queries` | [hotchpotch/arxiv-ir-simulated-search-queries](https://huggingface.co/datasets/hotchpotch/arxiv-ir-simulated-search-queries) | 1 | 2.88M |

## Source groups

The table below groups subsets by data family. `Rows` counts examples, not bytes or documents.

| Family | Subsets | Rows | Main form | Upstream/origin |
|---|---:|---:|---|---|
| NLLB bitext | 1,670 | 279.8M | `doc_doc` | [hotchpotch/nllb-sampled-500k](https://huggingface.co/datasets/hotchpotch/nllb-sampled-500k) plus [hotchpotch/nllb-english-bitext-hq](https://huggingface.co/datasets/hotchpotch/nllb-english-bitext-hq); English HQ overlaps removed from the sampled set. |

| Wikipedia retrieval and related-document data | 360 | 285.0M | `doc_doc`, `query_doc`, triplet | Wikipedia/FineWiki title-document, synthetic query, related paragraph/lead, and NLI conversions; original corpus family includes [wikimedia/wikipedia](https://huggingface.co/datasets/wikimedia/wikipedia). |

| Sentence Transformers multilingual and NLI data | 141 | 53.4M | `doc_doc`, `query_doc`, triplet | [OPUS-100](https://huggingface.co/datasets/sentence-transformers/parallel-sentences-opus-100), [WikiMatrix](https://huggingface.co/datasets/sentence-transformers/parallel-sentences-wikimatrix), [WikiTitles](https://huggingface.co/datasets/sentence-transformers/parallel-sentences-wikititles), [AllNLI](https://huggingface.co/datasets/sentence-transformers/all-nli), and [MIRACL](https://huggingface.co/datasets/sentence-transformers/miracl) releases via Sentence Transformers. |
| SWIM IR | 27 | 18.4M | `query_doc` | Synthetic Wikipedia-based IR via [nthakur/swim-ir-cross-lingual](https://huggingface.co/datasets/nthakur/swim-ir-cross-lingual) and [nthakur/swim-ir-monolingual](https://huggingface.co/datasets/nthakur/swim-ir-monolingual). |
| mMARCO hard negatives | 10 | 9.99M | `query_doc`, triplet | Multilingual MS MARCO / mMARCO triplets via [hotchpotch/mmarco-hard-negatives-reranker-filtered](https://huggingface.co/datasets/hotchpotch/mmarco-hard-negatives-reranker-filtered). |
| Instruction, code, math, and chat pairs | 10 | 6.77M | `query_doc` | NVIDIA post-training and code-reasoning prompt/response pairs converted to query-document format. |

## Mixed-source subset details

Two upstream datasets in this release are themselves mixtures of many source datasets. They are expanded below at the subset level rather than treated as one homogeneous source.

### Nomic subset details

This release maps selected splits from [nomic-ai/nomic-embed-unsupervised-data](https://huggingface.co/datasets/nomic-ai/nomic-embed-unsupervised-data) into separate subsets. Nomic's card describes the release as weakly supervised contrastive data for Nomic Embed models, with weakly related text pairs such as forum QA, title-body pairs, and news summarization pairs. Nomic links the technical report and the training code in [nomic-ai/contrastors](https://github.com/nomic-ai/contrastors). Where a more specific public source is available from the Nomic card, Sentence Transformers cards, or the LightOn source table, it is linked below.

| Nomic split / this subset | Rows | Form | Upstream/origin |
|---|---:|---|---|
| `agnews` | 417.8k | `qd` | [Nomic split](https://huggingface.co/datasets/nomic-ai/nomic-embed-unsupervised-data) via AG News title/description style data; related formatted ST card: [sentence-transformers/agnews](https://huggingface.co/datasets/sentence-transformers/agnews). |
| `altlex` | 106.5k | `dd` | [Nomic split](https://huggingface.co/datasets/nomic-ai/nomic-embed-unsupervised-data) via AltLex / English Wikipedia-to-Simple Wikipedia style pairs; see [sentence-transformers/altlex](https://huggingface.co/datasets/sentence-transformers/altlex). |
| `amazon_reviews` | 39.4M | `qd` | [Nomic split](https://huggingface.co/datasets/nomic-ai/nomic-embed-unsupervised-data) via Amazon review title/body pairs; related ST card: [sentence-transformers/amazon-reviews](https://huggingface.co/datasets/sentence-transformers/amazon-reviews). |
| `amazonqa` | 221.2k | `qd` | [Nomic split](https://huggingface.co/datasets/nomic-ai/nomic-embed-unsupervised-data) via AmazonQA question-answer pairs; related raw reference is [AmazonQA](https://github.com/amazonqa/amazonqa). |
| `ccnews` | 352.3k | `qd` | [Nomic split](https://huggingface.co/datasets/nomic-ai/nomic-embed-unsupervised-data) via CC-News title/article pairs; related HF source family: [vblagoje/cc_news](https://huggingface.co/datasets/vblagoje/cc_news). |

### LightOn subset details

This release uses three subsets from [lightonai/embeddings-pre-training](https://huggingface.co/datasets/lightonai/embeddings-pre-training). The LightOn dataset card states that the dataset was assembled to reproduce and build on the mGTE data recipe, exposes `query`/`document` plus cleaning columns, and provides a source link per subset. The rows below are the row counts in this release.

| LightOn subset | Rows | Form | Upstream/origin |
|---|---:|---|---|
| `beir_dbpedia` | 4.63M | `qd` | [LightOn subset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/lightonai/embeddings-pre-training) sourced from [BeIR/dbpedia-entity](https://huggingface.co/datasets/BeIR/dbpedia-entity). |
| `hermes` | 606.2k | `qd` | [LightOn subset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/lightonai/embeddings-pre-training) sourced from [teknium/OpenHermes-2.5](https://huggingface.co/datasets/teknium/OpenHermes-2.5). |
| `webfaq_eng` | 48.9M | `qd` | [LightOn subset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/lightonai/embeddings-pre-training) sourced from [PaDaS-Lab/webfaq](https://huggingface.co/datasets/PaDaS-Lab/webfaq) English WebFAQ. |

## Notes on selected sources

### NLLB bitext

The NLLB portion is split into two groups. [hotchpotch/nllb-english-bitext-hq](https://huggingface.co/datasets/hotchpotch/nllb-english-bitext-hq) contains English-centric high-quality bitext and documents its origin as NLLB plus CCMatrix. `hotchpotch/nllb-sampled-500k-without-hq` is derived from [hotchpotch/nllb-sampled-500k](https://huggingface.co/datasets/hotchpotch/nllb-sampled-500k); subsets containing `eng_Latn` were removed when the counterpart language was already covered by the English HQ bitext set. This leaves 1,575 subsets from the 1,640 source subsets.

### Wikipedia and FineWiki

The Wikipedia-related groups include multilingual query-document IR pairs, synthetic search-query pairs, FineWiki title-to-article pairs, related paragraph/lead document-document pairs, English Wikipedia simulated search-query pairs, and synthetic NLI. The original corpus family is Wikipedia/Wikimedia content, with some Hotchpotch datasets adding synthetic queries or related-document mining.

### News, web, scientific, and QA sources

FineWeb2-IR, FineWeb simulated search queries, CC-News, multilingual CC-News, arXiv, PubMed abstracts, LightOn, and Nomic sources provide broad-domain web/news/scientific/QA coverage. Several of these are synthetic retrieval conversions where a query-like text is paired with the document it should retrieve.

### Triplet sources

Triplet subsets come from FineWeb2-IR, mMARCO hard negatives, AllNLI, MIRACL, and synthetic Wikipedia NLI. These subsets include a `neg` column and carry the `__triplet` suffix in the subset name.

## Loading examples

```python
from datasets import load_dataset

ds = load_dataset(
    "hotchpotch/bekko-embedding-v1-unsupervised",
    "hotchpotch-wikipedia-multilingual-ir-pairs__enwiki-20251202-v1.1.0__qd",
    split="train",
)
print(ds.column_names)  # ['shared_id', 'query', 'pos']
```

```python
from datasets import load_dataset

ds = load_dataset(
    "hotchpotch/bekko-embedding-v1-unsupervised",
    "hotchpotch-fineweb2-ir__eng_Latn__qd__triplet",
    split="train",
)
print(ds.column_names)  # ['shared_id', 'query', 'pos', 'neg']
```

## License

This release follows the licenses and terms of the original upstream datasets. Because this dataset is an aggregation and normalization of many upstream sources, users are responsible for checking the license and usage constraints of each source group they use. For mixed-source datasets such as Nomic and LightOn, check the listed subset origin as well as the immediate upstream dataset card.

## Release notes

This dataset is published without extra metadata columns beyond the schemas described above. The release was checked against Hugging Face repository/parquet metadata to confirm row counts for all 2,452 subsets.
